# Devil Design approved duplicate migration

## Owner-approved outcome

110 groups approved; D031–D035 explicitly deferred. All survivor metadata is kept. No binding, identifier transfer or metadata update.

| Measure | Before | After |
|---|---:|---:|
| Global records |52,799|52,689|
| Devil Design records |608|498|
| Registry |635|745|

New/changed/rekeyed/unregistered removals =0/0/0/0. Five deferred Galaxy PETG groups (10 records) and378 unique/out-of-scope records remain unchanged. All code/EAN/refill-EAN bindings and unique values remain0; no transfers/losses.

## Deferred Rule 5 groups

D031–D035 remain unapproved and untouched. Full original keys/identity decompositions are retained in the JSON audit and historical report below; the running [duplicate backlog](duplicate-backlog.md) also lists them.

## Breaking catalog migration

The110 approved retired IDs disappear from published filaments.json. Existing Spoolman spools retain their local imported data. Spoolman does not consume retired_ids.json, redirect old catalog lookups or migrate stored external IDs. Other consumers must use the exact mapping. Rollback requires exact original-key reinstatement and baseline re-enrollment.

## Historical STEP 3 report — retained snapshot

The following report records the earlier unapproved115-group proposal and entire-brand stop. Its unapproved/count/stop statements are historical only: the newest owner decision approves110 groups and defers5, as recorded above and in the authoritative JSON root groups. It does not propose metadata changes or authorize the deferred groups.

# Devil Design — STEP 3 review only

## Verdict

**BLOCK automatic application for the entire brand.** No source/contract/docs edits, no apply, no commit, no push, no comments. All proposals below are unapproved. The 110 Rule-5-passing groups have NOT been applied either.

Current clean main / origin/main: `20348b2ad88723d6ab2a1c0ecf683e3733d1b3e2`. Read-only upstream preference snapshot: `8f1a99f9cda7a58ca3118d6bd28c707647caa3db`.

115 candidate groups: 115 Rule 1 survivors; no Rule 3/4/tie/Cartesian warning. Five Galaxy PETG groups fail Rule 5. Candidate-count order: Devil Design 115, Paramount3D 115; source slug tie-break. No subsequent brand was audited or applied.

| Count | Current / actual after audit | Hypothetical all 115 (INVALID until resolved) |
|---|---:|---:|
| Global compiled | 52,799 / 52,799 | 52,684 |
| Devil Design compiled | 608 / 608 | 493 |
| Registry | 635 / 635 | 750 |

Actual retired/new/changed identity/rekeyed/metadata changes = 0/0/0/0/0. Codes/EAN/refill-EAN bindings and unique values are 0 before and in the hypothetical projection; no transfer or loss. Other-material default check: candidate survivor density and scalar temperatures match/fall within the current official family product-card values; none flagged.

## Rule 5 blockers — exact mappings and baseline keys

Candidate name comparison removes the material token and finds a matching expanded name. Rule 5 independently compares ordered **line** and **color**. One source stores `Galaxy` in the color; the other stores it in the product line. No reviewed identity decomposition is supplied. Do not infer physical equality or silently install bindings.

## Current first-party evidence and unresolved metadata

The manufacturer downloads page currently links all five English product cards; fetched HTTP 200, SHA-256 and render/text snapshots are in `evidence/`. These are current family printing references, not proof of production lot or a wholesale package matrix. No proposed metadata changes.

| Family | Current density | Nozzle | Bed | Source |
|---|---:|---|---|---|
| PLA | 1.24 | 200–235 °C | 50–60 °C | [official card](https://devildesign.com/download/PLA_-_product_card.pdf) |
| PETG | 1.23 | 220–250 °C | 70–80 °C | [official card](https://devildesign.com/download/PET-G_-_product_card.pdf) |
| SILK | 1.23 | 225–245 °C | 50–60 °C | [official card](https://devildesign.com/download/SILK_-_product_card.pdf) |
| ASA | 1.07 | 230–240 °C | 90–100 °C | [official card](https://devildesign.com/download/ASA_-_product_card.pdf) |
| HIPS | 1.05 | 230–240 °C | 90–100 °C | [official card](https://devildesign.com/download/HIPS_-_product_card.pdf) |

- PET-G card page 2 explicitly marks Galaxy Super Blue, Galaxy Violet and Galaxy Black as 1.75 mm / 1 kg. Galaxy Red and Galaxy Green are not listed in that current PET-G card; their current PETG availability is unconfirmed, not disproven. Their appearance in the PLA card is NOT PETG evidence.
- PETG survivor density 1.23 matches the current card; retired proposals use 1.27. HIPS survivor 1.05 matches its current card; the other source uses 1.04. SILK survivor 1.23 matches its own PLA/PET-G blend card; the other source uses 1.24. These are recorded conflicts, not changes.
- Candidate survivors store scalar nozzle/bed values within current family ranges. Other definitions store ranges with different endpoints. Do not conflate scalar/range representation with proven formulation changes; retain both values below.
- All 115 groups differ in spool tare (260 vs 250 g). Current cards say approximately 250 g and a transparent polycarbonate spool. No newest-lot binding to these historical cells was found. Keep survivor tare/packaging untouched. “Printed cardboard” in the cards describes the outer box, NOT spool material.
- Current ASA/HIPS cards describe 800 g net; candidate historical cells are 1000 g on both sides. This is an additional historical/current-package evidence gap, not authority to change weight, retire unrelated records or generate 800 g records.
- HEX differences have no manufacturer HEX/lot proof; keep survivor HEX. Raw case-only differences are separately counted. Finish, translucency and document-link differences remain recorded.
- No newer-lot label/revision was found for the disputed packaging/tare. No Git-recency decision, guessed metadata or implicit Cartesian availability is used.
- Direct web-tool reopening of the ASA /255 and SILK /232 shop pages failed (redirect loop/cache miss); they are not used as authoritative updates. The current directly fetched manufacturer PDF cards are the verified evidence here.

| Conflict field | Groups |
|---|---:|
| spool_weight | 115 |
| color_hex | 115 |
| extruder_temp | 115 |
| extruder_temp_range | 115 |
| bed_temp | 115 |
| bed_temp_range | 115 |
| density | 60 |
| translucent | 3 |
| tds_url | 5 |
| finish | 10 |

HEX case-only: 1; actual value differences: 114. All original values are retained per group below and in JSON.
